## Self-tests

Run with `/sb selftest [filter]` (works over RCON). A long run is spread across ticks; `/sb selftest report` prints its result, and `scripts/rcon.ps1` polls that for you.

- `abilities.handlers-registered`
- `abilities.mana-disintegrator`
- `accessories.dungeon-gear`

## Dials

Change live with `/sb dial set <key> <value>`; `/sb dial reset <key>` returns to the default.

| Key | Default | Current | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `abilities.enabled` | true | true | flag | Master switch for item ability triggers |
| `abilities.flash-ticks` | 40 | 40 | integer | How long the '-50 Mana (Ability)' action bar text stays |

| `ui.tab-refresh-ticks` | 40 | 40 | integer | Ticks between tab list header/footer/name updates |
| `ui.vanilla-advancements` | false | false | flag | Let vanilla grant advancements (shows 'Advancement Made!' toasts) |
| `ui.vanilla-recipe-toasts` | false | false | flag | Let vanilla unlock recipe-book recipes (shows 'New Recipe(s) Unlocked!' toasts) |
| `vitals.action-bar` | true | true | flag | Show the health / defense / mana action bar |
| `vitals.combat-seconds` | 5 | 5 | number | Seconds after losing health during which the player counts as in combat |
| `vitals.death-coin-loss` | 0.5 | 0.5 | number | Fraction of the purse lost on death outside safe zones (research 01 §5: half) |
| `vitals.death-safe-private-island` | true | true | flag | Dying on a private island costs no coins |
| `vitals.disable-hunger` | true | true | flag | Keep the hunger bar full (the genre has no hunger) |
| `vitals.heart-scale-max` | 40 | 40 | number | Most vanilla half-hearts shown as max health grows (20 = 10 hearts at 100 HP, 40 = 20 hearts) |
| `vitals.mana-regen-percent` | 2 | 2 | number | Percent of max mana regenerated per second before Mana Regen (research 01 §7: 2) |
| `vitals.regen-interval-seconds` | 2 | 2 | number | Seconds between health regen ticks (API javadoc: 2s) |
| `vitals.regen-multiplier` | 1 | 1 | number | Multiplier on (1.5 + max/100) * HealthRegen/100 per regen tick |
| `vitals.regen-out-of-combat-multiplier` | 1 | 1 | number | Extra regen multiplier while out of combat. TUNE: research 01 §6.1 says the June-2026 game doubles it (2.0) |
| `vitals.vanilla-damage-net` | true | true | flag | Catch vanilla damage that combat did not route and apply it to custom health (never let vanilla kill) |
| `weapons.ability-damage-multiplier` | 1 | 1 | number | Multiplier on every weapon ability's base damage (1.0 = research numbers) |
| `weapons.adaptive-archer-percent` | 10 | 10 | number | Adaptive Blade (Archer): a melee hit makes the foe take this % more from your arrows for 5s (reference: 10%) |
| `weapons.adaptive-healer-share-percent` | 33 | 33 | number | Adaptive Blade (Healer): % of your life-steal healing shared with party members within 10 blocks in a dungeon (reference: 33%) |
| `weapons.adaptive-mage-mana-percent` | 25 | 25 | number | Adaptive Blade (Mage): extra mana per dungeon melee hit, as % of the 5 + 1% max mana a melee hit restores (reference: 25%) |
| `weapons.arrow-poisons` | true | true | flag | Arrow poisons in the inventory are used up on every shot |
| `weapons.arrow-speed` | 3 | 3 | number | Launch speed of shortbow arrows (vanilla full draw = 3.0) |
| `weapons.bow-melee-damage` | false | false | flag | TUNE: punching with a bow uses the bow's Damage stat (false = bows count as fists in melee) |
| `weapons.catacombs-requirement` | dungeon | dungeon | text | Where a weapon's 'Catacombs Level N' requirement applies: dungeon = only inside a dungeon run (reference), everywhere = all islands, none = never |
| `weapons.class-ability-weapons` | true | true | flag | Right-clicking a class-ability weapon (Highlord & co, Earthen/Adaptive Blade, Fel Sword) inside a dungeon uses your class ability |
| `weapons.cleave-max-targets` | 6 | 6 | integer | TUNE: max extra mobs one Cleave hit can splash |
| `weapons.cleave-percent` | 25 | 25 | number | TUNE: % of a melee hit that Cleave splashes onto nearby mobs (reference says only 'a portion') |
| `weapons.cone-range` | 8 | 8 | number | TUNE: reach of cone abilities (Wyrm's Fury, Frost Breath) |
| `weapons.doomcall-strength-multiplier` | 1.5 | 1.5 | number | Doomcall multiplies the weapon's Strength by this while active (reference: 1.5x) |
| `weapons.dungeon-fallback-items` | true | true | flag | Register the dungeon weapons this module ships when the dungeons module did not (needs a restart) |
| `weapons.emerald-blade-coefficient` | 2.5 | 2.5 | number | TUNE: Emerald Blade bonus damage = coefficient x purse^(1/4) (reference formula as remembered: 2.5) |
| `weapons.emerald-blade-purse-cap` | 2000000000 | 2000000000 | number | Purse counted by the Emerald Blade (reference: 2B) |
| `weapons.ether-transmission-range` | 57 | 57 | number | Phasewarp (Ether Transmission) reach in blocks before Transmission Tuners (research 06: 57) |
| `weapons.explosion-radius-scale` | 1 | 1 | number | Scale on every explosive arrow / bow blast radius |
| `weapons.grappling-cooldown` | 2 | 2 | number | Grappling Hook cooldown in seconds (reference: 2s) |
| `weapons.grappling-strength` | 1 | 1 | number | TUNE: Grappling Hook pull multiplier |
| `weapons.halberd-streak-reset` | 3 | 3 | number | TUNE: seconds without throwing before the halberd throw streak resets |
| `weapons.homing-range` | 10 | 10 | number | Blocks within which homing arrows pick a target (reference: 10 for dragon tracer) |
| `weapons.ignite-percent-per-second` | 3 | 3 | number | TUNE: Flaming Sword burn = this % of the hit's damage every second while alight (Fire Aspect I rate) |
| `weapons.infinite-quiver-percent-per-level` | 3 | 3 | number | Infinite Quiver: % chance per level that a shot takes no arrow when this module takes the arrow (research 01: 3%, X = 30%) |
| `weapons.multi-arrow-spread` | 7 | 7 | number | TUNE: degrees between arrows of a multi-arrow shot |
| `weapons.pet-perks` | true | true | flag | Weapon-side pet perks: Ghoul (souls, raised souls' cost/damage/health), Eerie (Fear damage vs spooky, Fearcreasing) |
| `weapons.quiver-arrow` | true | true | flag | Holding a drawn bow with no loose arrows puts a Quiver Arrow in hotbar slot 9 so the bow draws straight from the quiver (reference) |
| `weapons.raider-coins-per-kill` | 20 | 20 | number | Raider Sword bonus coins per monster kill (reference: 20, Combat 10+) |
| `weapons.rose-hop-bonus` | 0.5 | 0.5 | number | TUNE: extra damage per hop of the rose abilities (0.5 = +50% per foe already hit) |
| `weapons.shortbow-min-cooldown` | 0.1 | 0.1 | number | Floor (seconds) for a shortbow's shot cooldown after Bonus Attack Speed (base / (1 + AS/100), AS capped at 100) |
| `weapons.shortbow-needs-arrows` | true | true | flag | Shortbows consume an arrow per shot (inventory, then quiver) |
| `weapons.slayer-fallback-items` | true | true | flag | Register the slayer weapons this module ships when the slayer module did not (needs a restart) |
| `weapons.soul-damage-multiplier` | 1 | 1 | number | TUNE: a raised soul hits once a second for its monster's Damage stat times this |
| `weapons.soul-drop-chance` | 100 | 100 | number | % chance a kill with a Raise Souls blade drops its soul (reference: always); the Ghoul pet's '+a% more often' multiplies it |
| `weapons.soul-health` | 40 | 40 | number | TUNE: vanilla health of a raised soul (only stray blows and the environment hurt it) |
| `weapons.soul-lifetime` | 20 | 20 | number | TUNE: seconds a freed soul waits on the ground to be absorbed (Raise Souls) |
| `weapons.soulflow-judgement-beam` | true | true | flag | Judgement Beam needs 1 Soulstream (reference Soulflow); short = the click is a normal shot. The soulflow module charges it |
| `weapons.spooky-event-id` | spooky_festival | spooky_festival | text | Calendar event id during which Fright Night (Ghoul Buster) is active |
| `weapons.sting-seconds` | 30 | 30 | number | Stinger Bow: seconds of +20 Speed / +30 Strength on a teammate you shoot (reference: 30) |
| `weapons.zombie-sword-recharge` | 15 | 15 | number | TUNE: seconds for one Zombie-sword heal charge to return |
| `world.auto-build` | true | true | flag | Build islands whose generator version changed when the server starts. |
| `world.boot-layouts` | true | true | flag | On first boot, compute missing island layouts before NPCs spawn (slower start, NPCs in place at once). |
| `world.build-budget-ms` | 12 | 12 | integer | Milliseconds per tick spent placing island blocks (keeps 20 TPS while building). |
| `world.build-preload-chunks` | 6 | 6 | integer | How many chunks ahead the builder loads asynchronously. |
| `world.build-progress-percent` | 5 | 5 | integer | Report build progress every N percent. |
| `world.deny-message-seconds` | 2 | 2 | integer | Minimum seconds between 'you can't enter/build here' messages per player. |
| `world.discover-titles` | true | true | flag | Show NEW AREA DISCOVERED the first time a player enters a zone. |
| `world.gather-collections` | true | true | flag | Blocks a player breaks by hand on a gather island (BUILD zones of world.gather-islands) go straight to their inventory as natural loot, so collections and sacks count them. Blocks a player placed never count. |
| `world.gather-islands` | private_island | private_island | text | Comma-separated islands whose BUILD-zone breaks count as gathering (see world.gather-collections). |
| `world.join-location` | home | home | text | Where players appear on join: 'home' (private island, else hub) or 'last' (where they logged out). |
| `world.no-trampling` | true | true | flag | Players and mobs never trample farmland in game worlds. |
| `world.portal-cooldown-seconds` | 3 | 3 | integer | Seconds before a portal will warp the same player again. |
| `world.protect-containers` | true | true | flag | Deny vanilla use of chests/furnaces/anvils/etc. outside BUILD zones (modules that handle a block still see the click). |
| `world.time-sync-seconds` | 30 | 30 | integer | How often (seconds) island time of day is set from the SkyBlock calendar. |
| `world.zone-interval-ticks` | 10 | 10 | integer | How often (ticks) each player's zone/island is re-checked. |
